+# Issue5442: owned browser form to SQLite endpoint
+
+Eight actual DOM form submissions all returned HTTP200 and visibly showed
+`Dispatcher accepted`. A different Python process using a fresh read-only SQLite
+connection confirmed only C001/C005. The six seeded wrong-target, no-op and
+stale-precondition effects remained UNKNOWN. Each received exactly one new
+fresh-version repair submission; all six repair effects were CONFIRMED. The
+original initial decisions remain unchanged.
+
+Result: **PASS_APPLICATION_ENDPOINT_SCOPED**. This is a controlled application
+construction, not a general GUI success rate or a promoted runtime authority.
+The separate raw-only auditor reconciled14 operations, their SQL backups,
+56 UI trace entries and14 HTTP POST200 records; ten copied-raw mutations were
+refused, including actual event target and the integer/boolean alias.
+
+| Cases | Seeded endpoint behavior | Initial A/B effect | Initial decision | New repair |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| C001/C005 | correct target and preversion | A0→1, desired value; B0 empty | CONFIRMED | not needed |
+| C002/C006 | actual target B | A0 empty; B0→1, desired value | UNKNOWN | A0→1 desired; B1→2 empty |
+| C003/C007 | no-op | A/B0 empty | UNKNOWN | A0→1 desired; B0 empty |
+| C004/C008 | precondition changed before write | A1→2 desired; B0 empty | UNKNOWN | A2→3 desired; B0 empty |
+
+## Evidence and first outcomes
+
+The source freeze SHA256 is
+`3d79ff351af775bda98bbaab5247010edb1e9936b9419146539f610c88baa11f`.
+It preceded the sole primary server startup at2026-10-03T03:21:03Z. This study
+used CPython3.11.9 / SQLite3.45.1 / Windows with one owned hidden Codex IAB tab,
+DOM locators through CUA only. No repository runtime, external site, additional
+worker, native desktop input, container or experimental model API was invoked.
+Browser graphics and coordinating assistant settings are unmeasured.
+
+The package retains original source bytes under `retained-source/`; `.py`/`.js`
+files have an added `.txt` extension to avoid automatic execution. Source names
+in the original FREEZE.json are unchanged. `SOURCE_MAP.json` maps them to these
+plain-text files. To inspect the pure auditor, materialize all original names
+plus FREEZE.json in a new private directory and run auditor.py against the
+retained run-01. This reads retained data and does not start the app or repeat
+GUI operations. No original allocation is available for a primary replay.
+
+`run-01/` retains the first observations, prestate, coherent SQL backups, final
+database, UI traces, initial/final JPEGs, source-load binding, process identity,
+stdout/stderr, command exit receipts and resource cleanup. SHA-256 identifies
+bytes, not authenticity. The endpoint is authored by this worker; distinct
+process/connection observation is not independent observer trust.
+
+First construction failed compilation because two multiline return expressions
+lacked parentheses. `construction-01/` preserves the original app source,
+compiler error and first failure. Ordinary construction repair added those
+parentheses. `construction-02/` then passed8 seeded cases,8 duplicate guards,
+6 stale repair guards and6 fresh repair checks before the source freeze, with
+zero GUI actions/primary server starts. These unit checks are not GUI evidence.
+
+First CUA actor setup rejected eval (`Code generation from strings disallowed`).
+That occurred before goto or any submit. The literal async function was then
+defined through CUA and compared to the frozen actor source text, changing only
+the loading method. Both records remain. The actor and primary app source were
+not edited after freeze, and no primary operation was retried.
+
+After all fourteen completed operations, read-only observation/backup and proof
+capture, the owned tab was closed. The exact server PID and command line were
+checked before Stop-Process; its observed exit was-1 (intentional termination,
+not a graceful-shutdown claim). The launch observer itself exited0. No shared
+input/display lease or main apply lock was held.
+
+## Decision and limits
+
+H/T/D/C/U and the prospective sequence are preserved in PLAN.md and
+UI_PROTOCOL.md; the Japanese variable/type/unit table is VARIABLES.md, all in
+retained-source. The initial-only dispatch baseline yields eight accepted
+returns despite six seeded semantic failures. Final text alone would also miss
+the stale-precondition cases. The binding predicate adds target/preversion and
+actual saved state; bounded repair establishes a new explicitly different
+intent using a guarded SQL transaction.
+
+This advances T8's fixture-only boundary to an actual controlled HTML form and
+committed SQLite state visible to another connection. It does not establish
+arbitrary application fidelity, observer authenticity, multi-user/concurrent
+safety, crash/power-loss durability, physical input occupancy/release,

+"""Controlled local HTML form and persisted endpoint; no external dependencies."""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import html
+import json
+import os
+import sqlite3
+import time
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def initialize(path, scenarios):
+    if path.exists():
+        raise ValueError('existing database; no reset/restart')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.executescript('''
+        CREATE TABLE documents(room TEXT, target TEXT, version INTEGER, value TEXT,
+                               PRIMARY KEY(room,target));
+        CREATE TABLE events(room TEXT, phase TEXT, nonce TEXT UNIQUE,
+          requested_target TEXT, actual_target TEXT, applied_pre INTEGER,
+          applied_post INTEGER, written INTEGER, text TEXT, observed_ns INTEGER,
+          PRIMARY KEY(room,phase));
+        CREATE TABLE requests(sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+          method TEXT, path TEXT, status INTEGER, observed_ns INTEGER);
+        ''')
+        for item in scenarios:
+            db.executemany('INSERT INTO documents VALUES(?,?,0,?)',
+                           [(item['trial_id'], 'A', ''), (item['trial_id'], 'B', '')])
+        db.commit()
+
+
+def snapshot(path, room):
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        return {target: {'version': version, 'value': value}
+                for target, version, value in db.execute(
+                    'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=? ORDER BY target', (room,))}
+
+
+def apply(path, scenario, phase, value, expected=None):
+    room = scenario['trial_id']
+    nonce = scenario['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
+        if db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE room=? AND phase=?', (room, phase)).fetchone():
+            raise ValueError('duplicate operation')
+        state = {t: (v, text) for t, v, text in db.execute(
+            'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=?', (room,))}
+        if phase == 'repair':
+            if expected != {t: state[t][0] for t in ('A', 'B')}:
+                raise ValueError('fresh-version comparison failed')
+            pre = state['A'][0]
+            if state['B'][1]:
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', ('', room, 'B'))
+            db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, room, 'A'))
+            target, written, post = 'A', 1, pre + 1
+        else:
+            target = 'B' if scenario['mode'] == 'wrong_target' else 'A'
+            if scenario['mode'] == 'stale_precondition':
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, 'A'))
+            pre = db.execute('SELECT version FROM documents WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, target)).fetchone()[0]
+            written = int(scenario['mode'] != 'noop')
+            post = pre + written
+            if written:
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=? WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, post, room, target))
+        db.execute('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
+            (room, phase, nonce, 'A', target, pre, post, written, value, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+        db.commit()
+    return nonce
+
+
+def serve(out):
+    freeze = json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())
+    for name, digest in freeze['files'].items():
+        if hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
+            raise ValueError('source freeze drift:' + name)
+    scenarios = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+    by_id = {r['trial_id']: r for r in scenarios}
+    database = out / 'endpoint.sqlite'
+    initialize(database, scenarios)
+    (out / 'initial-database.sha256').write_text(hashlib.sha256(database.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + '\n')
+
+    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+        def request_log(self, status):
+            with closing(sqlite3.connect(database)) as db:
+                db.execute('INSERT INTO requests(method,path,status,observed_ns) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',
+                           (self.command, self.path, status, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+                db.commit()
+
+        def send_page(self, body, status=200):
+            self.request_log(status)
+            data = ('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Endpoint receipt test</title>'
+                '<style>body{font:16px system-ui;margin:30px;color:#172230}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}'
+                'th,td{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #ccd5dd;text-align:left}input,button{font:inherit;padding:9px}'
+                '.card{display:inline-block;vertical-align:top;padding:15px;background:#edf2f6;margin:8px 8px 20px 0}'
+                'h1{font-size:25px}h2{font-size:20px}.note{color:#536271}</style></head><body>' + body + '</body></html>').encode()
+            self.send_response(status)
+            self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8')
+            self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(data)))
+            self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; base-uri 'none'")
+            self.end_headers()
+            self.wfile.write(data)
+
+        def document_cards(self, room):
+            current = snapshot(database, room)
+            return ''.join('<section class="card"><h2>Document ' + target + '</h2><p>Version ' + str(row['version']) +
+                '</p><p>Saved value: ' + html.escape(row['value'] or '(empty)') + '</p></section>' for target, row in current.items())
+
+        def dashboard(self):
+            initial_file, repair_file = out / 'initial-observation.json', out / 'repair-observation.json'
+            initial = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(initial_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if initial_file.exists() else {}
+            repair = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(repair_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if repair_file.exists() else {}
+            rows = []
+            for scenario in scenarios:
+                room = scenario['trial_id']
+                state = snapshot(database, room)
+                observed = initial.get(room, {})
+                repaired = repair.get(room, {})
+                recovery = '<a href="/repair/' + room + '">Repair ' + room + '</a>' if observed.get('semantic_status') == 'UNKNOWN' and not repaired else ''
+                rows.append('<tr><td><a href="/trial/' + room + '">Trial ' + room + '</a></td><td>' +
+                    html.escape(scenario['desired_text']) + '</td><td>' + observed.get('dispatch_status', 'not run') +
+                    '</td><td>' + observed.get('semantic_status', 'not observed') + '</td><td>' +
+                    repaired.get('semantic_status', recovery or 'not needed') + '</td><td>' + html.escape(state['A']['value'] or '(empty)') + '</td></tr>')
+            return '<h1>Endpoint receipt experiment</h1><p class="note">Private test records. Initial decisions remain unchanged after repair.</p>' +
+                '<table><tr><th>Trial</th><th>Goal value for A</th><th>Dispatch</th><th>Initial endpoint</th><th>Fresh repair</th><th>Saved A now</th></tr>' + ''.join(rows) + '</table>'
+
+        def editor(self, room, phase):
+            state = snapshot(database, room)
+            nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            hidden = ''.join('<input type="hidden" name="' + key + '" value="' + html.escape(str(value)) + '">' for key, value in {
+                'room': room, 'phase': phase, 'nonce': nonce, 'target': 'A',
+                'version_A': state['A']['version'], 'version_B': state['B']['version']}.items())
+            return '<h1>' + ('Fresh repair ' if phase == 'repair' else 'Trial ') + room + '</h1><p>Target: Document A</p>' +
+                self.document_cards(room) + '<form action="/apply" method="post">' + hidden +
+                '<label for="value">New value</label><br><input id="value" name="value" maxlength="128" required><button type="submit">' +
+                ('Repair safely' if phase == 'repair' else 'Save') + '</button></form><p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>'
+
+        def do_GET(self):
+            path = urlparse(self.path).path
+            if path == '/':
+                self.send_page(self.dashboard())
+            elif path.startswith('/trial/') or path.startswith('/repair/'):
+                room = path.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
+                if room not in by_id:
+                    self.send_page('Unknown trial', 404)
+                    return
+                phase = 'repair' if path.startswith('/repair/') else 'initial'
+                self.send_page(self.editor(room, phase))
+            else:
+                self.send_page('Not found', 404)
+
+        def do_POST(self):
+            if self.path != '/apply' or self.headers.get('Origin') != 'http://127.0.0.1:' + str(self.server.server_port):
+                self.send_page('Refused origin or route', 403)
+                return
+            count = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', '0'))
+            if not 0 < count <= 2048:
+                self.send_page('Refused body size', 400)
+                return
+            fields = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(self.rfile.read(count).decode(), keep_blank_values=True).items()}
+            room, phase = fields.get('room'), fields.get('phase')
+            if room not in by_id or phase not in ('initial', 'repair') or fields.get('target') != 'A' or not 0 < len(fields.get('value', '')) <= 128:
+                self.send_page('Refused fields', 400)
+                return
+            wanted_nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            if fields.get('nonce') != wanted_nonce:
+                self.send_page('Refused nonce', 400)
+                return
+            try:
+                expected = {t: int(fields['version_' + t]) for t in ('A', 'B')}
+                apply(database, by_id[room], phase, fields['value'], expected)
+            except (ValueError, KeyError):
+                self.send_page('Refused duplicate or stale repair', 409)
+                return
+            self.send_page('<h1>Request processed</h1><p role="status">Dispatcher accepted</p><p>Trial ' + room +
+                '</p>' + self.document_cards(room) + '<p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>')
+
+    server = HTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), Handler)
+    metadata = {'pid': os.getpid(), 'port': server.server_port, 'host': '127.0.0.1',
+                'started_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'freeze_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                'sqlite_version': sqlite3.sqlite_version, 'instance_id': freeze['study']}
+    with (out / 'server.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+        json.dump(metadata, handle, sort_keys=True, indent=2)
+    server.serve_forever()
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    serve(args.out)

+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import html
+import json
+import os
+import sqlite3
+import time
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def initialize(path, scenarios):
+    if path.exists():
+        raise ValueError('existing database; no reset/restart')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.executescript('''
+        CREATE TABLE documents(room TEXT, target TEXT, version INTEGER, value TEXT,
+                               PRIMARY KEY(room,target));
+        CREATE TABLE events(room TEXT, phase TEXT, nonce TEXT UNIQUE,
+          requested_target TEXT, actual_target TEXT, applied_pre INTEGER,
+          applied_post INTEGER, written INTEGER, text TEXT, observed_ns INTEGER,
+          PRIMARY KEY(room,phase));
+        CREATE TABLE requests(sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+          method TEXT, path TEXT, status INTEGER, observed_ns INTEGER);
+        ''')
+        for item in scenarios:
+            db.executemany('INSERT INTO documents VALUES(?,?,0,?)',
+                           [(item['trial_id'], 'A', ''), (item['trial_id'], 'B', '')])
+        db.commit()
+
+
+def snapshot(path, room):
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        return {target: {'version': version, 'value': value}
+                for target, version, value in db.execute(
+                    'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=? ORDER BY target', (room,))}
+
+
+def apply(path, scenario, phase, value, expected=None):
+    room = scenario['trial_id']
+    nonce = scenario['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
+        if db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE room=? AND phase=?', (room, phase)).fetchone():
+            raise ValueError('duplicate operation')
+        state = {t: (v, text) for t, v, text in db.execute(
+            'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=?', (room,))}
+        if phase == 'repair':
+            if expected != {t: state[t][0] for t in ('A', 'B')}:
+                raise ValueError('fresh-version comparison failed')
+            pre = state['A'][0]

+            db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, room, 'A'))
+            target, written, post = 'A', 1, pre + 1
+        else:
+            target = 'B' if scenario['mode'] == 'wrong_target' else 'A'
+            if scenario['mode'] == 'stale_precondition':
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, 'A'))
+            pre = db.execute('SELECT version FROM documents WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, target)).fetchone()[0]
+            written = int(scenario['mode'] != 'noop')
+            post = pre + written
+            if written:
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=? WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, post, room, target))
+        db.execute('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
+            (room, phase, nonce, 'A', target, pre, post, written, value, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+        db.commit()
+    return nonce
+
+
+def serve(out):
+    freeze = json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())
+    for name, digest in freeze['files'].items():
+        if hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
+            raise ValueError('source freeze drift:' + name)
+    scenarios = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+    by_id = {r['trial_id']: r for r in scenarios}
+    database = out / 'endpoint.sqlite'
+    initialize(database, scenarios)
+    (out / 'initial-database.sha256').write_text(hashlib.sha256(database.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + '\n')
+
+    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+        def request_log(self, status):
+            with closing(sqlite3.connect(database)) as db:
+                db.execute('INSERT INTO requests(method,path,status,observed_ns) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',
+                           (self.command, self.path, status, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+                db.commit()
+
+        def send_page(self, body, status=200):
+            self.request_log(status)
+            data = ('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Endpoint receipt test</title>'
+                '<style>body{font:16px system-ui;margin:30px;color:#172230}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}'
+                'th,td{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #ccd5dd;text-align:left}input,button{font:inherit;padding:9px}'
+                '.card{display:inline-block;vertical-align:top;padding:15px;background:#edf2f6;margin:8px 8px 20px 0}'
+                'h1{font-size:25px}h2{font-size:20px}.note{color:#536271}</style></head><body>' + body + '</body></html>').encode()
+            self.send_response(status)
+            self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8')
+            self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(data)))
+            self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; base-uri 'none'")
+            self.end_headers()
+            self.wfile.write(data)
+
+        def document_cards(self, room):
+            current = snapshot(database, room)
+            return ''.join('<section class="card"><h2>Document ' + target + '</h2><p>Version ' + str(row['version']) +
+                '</p><p>Saved value: ' + html.escape(row['value'] or '(empty)') + '</p></section>' for target, row in current.items())
+
+        def dashboard(self):
+            initial_file, repair_file = out / 'initial-observation.json', out / 'repair-observation.json'
+            initial = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(initial_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if initial_file.exists() else {}
+            repair = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(repair_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if repair_file.exists() else {}
+            rows = []
+            for scenario in scenarios:
+                room = scenario['trial_id']
+                state = snapshot(database, room)
+                observed = initial.get(room, {})
+                repaired = repair.get(room, {})
+                recovery = '<a href="/repair/' + room + '">Repair ' + room + '</a>' if observed.get('semantic_status') == 'UNKNOWN' and not repaired else ''
+                rows.append('<tr><td><a href="/trial/' + room + '">Trial ' + room + '</a></td><td>' +
+                    html.escape(scenario['desired_text']) + '</td><td>' + observed.get('dispatch_status', 'not run') +
+                    '</td><td>' + observed.get('semantic_status', 'not observed') + '</td><td>' +
+                    repaired.get('semantic_status', recovery or 'not needed') + '</td><td>' + html.escape(state['A']['value'] or '(empty)') + '</td></tr>')
+            return ('<h1>Endpoint receipt experiment</h1><p class="note">Private test records. Initial decisions remain unchanged after repair.</p>' +
+                '<table><tr><th>Trial</th><th>Goal value for A</th><th>Dispatch</th><th>Initial endpoint</th><th>Fresh repair</th><th>Saved A now</th></tr>' + ''.join(rows) + '</table>')
+
+        def editor(self, room, phase):
+            state = snapshot(database, room)
+            nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            hidden = ''.join('<input type="hidden" name="' + key + '" value="' + html.escape(str(value)) + '">' for key, value in {
+                'room': room, 'phase': phase, 'nonce': nonce, 'target': 'A',
+                'version_A': state['A']['version'], 'version_B': state['B']['version']}.items())
+            return ('<h1>' + ('Fresh repair ' if phase == 'repair' else 'Trial ') + room + '</h1><p>Target: Document A</p>' +
+                self.document_cards(room) + '<form action="/apply" method="post">' + hidden +
+                '<label for="value">New value</label><br><input id="value" name="value" maxlength="128" required><button type="submit">' +
+                ('Repair safely' if phase == 'repair' else 'Save') + '</button></form><p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>')
+
+        def do_GET(self):
+            path = urlparse(self.path).path
+            if path == '/':
+                self.send_page(self.dashboard())
+            elif path.startswith('/trial/') or path.startswith('/repair/'):
+                room = path.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
+                if room not in by_id:
+                    self.send_page('Unknown trial', 404)
+                    return
+                phase = 'repair' if path.startswith('/repair/') else 'initial'
+                self.send_page(self.editor(room, phase))
+            else:
+                self.send_page('Not found', 404)
+
+        def do_POST(self):
+            if self.path != '/apply' or self.headers.get('Origin') != 'http://127.0.0.1:' + str(self.server.server_port):
+                self.send_page('Refused origin or route', 403)
+                return
+            count = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', '0'))
+            if not 0 < count <= 2048:
+                self.send_page('Refused body size', 400)
+                return
+            fields = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(self.rfile.read(count).decode(), keep_blank_values=True).items()}
+            room, phase = fields.get('room'), fields.get('phase')
+            if room not in by_id or phase not in ('initial', 'repair') or fields.get('target') != 'A' or not 0 < len(fields.get('value', '')) <= 128:
+                self.send_page('Refused fields', 400)
+                return
+            wanted_nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            if fields.get('nonce') != wanted_nonce:
+                self.send_page('Refused nonce', 400)
+                return
+            try:
+                expected = {t: int(fields['version_' + t]) for t in ('A', 'B')}
+                apply(database, by_id[room], phase, fields['value'], expected)
+            except (ValueError, KeyError):
+                self.send_page('Refused duplicate or stale repair', 409)
+                return
+            self.send_page('<h1>Request processed</h1><p role="status">Dispatcher accepted</p><p>Trial ' + room +
+                '</p>' + self.document_cards(room) + '<p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>')
+
+    server = HTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), Handler)
+    metadata = {'pid': os.getpid(), 'port': server.server_port, 'host': '127.0.0.1',
+                'started_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'freeze_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                'sqlite_version': sqlite3.sqlite_version, 'instance_id': freeze['study']}
+    with (out / 'server.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+        json.dump(metadata, handle, sort_keys=True, indent=2)
+    server.serve_forever()
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    serve(args.out)

+# Application-bound endpoint construction for Issue5442
+
+H: scoped DOM dispatch success does not imply the requested SQLite target and
+precondition/effect. A fresh endpoint observer can distinguish them and bounded
+fresh-version recovery can establish a new, explicitly different repair receipt.
+T: one owned hidden Codex IAB tab and one private loopback HTML/SQLite app;
+8 rooms (2 desired strings x valid/wrong-target/noop/stale-precondition).
+Each gets exactly1 initial DOM form submit, not a simulator/candidate replay.
+Read SQLite through a separate process/connection; preserve first8 results.
+For the6 UNKNOWN rooms, a new guarded repair intent uses freshly observed A/B
+versions, one repair submit max, atomically restoring only fixture-owned B and
+writing A. Valid rooms get no repair. Original actions remain UNKNOWN forever.
+D: PASS_APPLICATION_ENDPOINT_SCOPED if all8 initial UI submissions return and
+show accepted, the independent persisted-state observer confirms2 and refuses6,
+then6 fresh-version repairs satisfy their separately declared predicates with
+no extra/missing operations. STOP/HOLD on missing identity/source/effect/trace,
+unexpected UI, unknown submit outcome or a duplicate; preserve partial data.
+C: same form/control/target A and scoped namespace, source, observer and desired
+texts; only seeded endpoint modes differ. Authority is only these authored
+SQLite rows. Namespace/target/preversion/text, nonces and phase are explicit.
+U: actual controlled HTML form and SQLite commit/read visibility, not arbitrary
+app observer authenticity, human/task safety, physical desktop input/release,
+durability under power loss, generic concurrent correctness, performance,
+latency-matched efficacy, model efficiency, promoted runtime or public MCP.
+
+Private browser owner: this worker; IAB browser2/tab1, DOM-only actions via CUA.
+Loopback server binds127.0.0.1 at an OS-assigned port, one process, stdlib only.
+No external site/data, shared display/container/GPU/model lease or extra worker.
+Graphics provenance is unmeasured; no training/inference job is invoked.
+Initial DB absent, source/actor/plan/predicates frozen before server startup.
+Initial first source/output remains immutable; no retry/restart/reset of this DB.
+T7/T8 formal allocations, hashes, outcomes and source stay untouched.
+Prospective main reference:6da2b492b9c2a9d76e5c54d35a0ae50b7b49edde.
+There is no imported repository runtime; app source and event-to-predicate
+transfer are the construction question, not kernel integration/adoption proof.

+# Prospective owned-browser protocol
+
+All browser interactions use mcp__cua_repl and the existing semanticTab binding
+(owned IAB browser2/tab1). Ordinary Node file I/O writes the trace and reads this
+actor source only; it never queries endpoint data, HTTP, other tabs or a browser
+control library. eval of actor.js defines functions; invocation uses CUA only.
+
+1. Freeze app/observer/auditor/actor/scenarios/plan/protocol and variables.
+2. Start one stdlib loopback process in run-01, preserve PID/port/stdout/stderr.
+   No primary restart/reset/replay. Record initial empty SQL state before any GET.
+3. Initialize trace once with exclusive file creation. Load actor.js once.
+4. Navigate the owned tab to the server root. Observe a fresh full DOM snapshot.
+5. From dashboard invoke runEndpointTrial for C001..C008, in that order, passing
+   only trial ID, desired text and initial phase. Each function observes editor,
+   fills New value, observes intent, records nonce before exactly one Save,
+   observes Request processed/Dispatcher accepted, then returns/observes dashboard.
+   A consumed key is registered before submit; any unexpected result stops this
+   primary operation. No blind retry or source change after freeze.
+6. Run observer.py --phase initial once in a different Python process; preserve
+   raw rows and coherent SQLite backup. Initial decisions are permanent.
+7. Reload dashboard once so it shows observer decisions and Repair Cxxx links.
+   For six UNKNOWN rooms only (C002/3/4/6/7/8), invoke the same frozen actor with
+   phase repair. Each editor is freshly loaded and displays A/B values/versions.
+   Its version-bearing form authorizes one atomic comparison/restore/write on
+   fixture-owned rows only. No repair for C001/5. Max one repair per failed room.
+8. Run observer.py --phase repair once; save its independent SQL backup. Run the
+   separate raw-only auditor on immutable raw/backup/trace (not app simulation).
+9. Reload root for final proof screenshot, save JPEG to outputs and research raw.
+   Preserve all first outcomes, including construction SyntaxError. Stop only
+   the identified owned server, record process exit, and close only owned tab.
+
+Source preflight/ordinary unit repairs are allowed before this source freeze.
+If a GUI submit becomes uncertain, save partial evidence and HOLD this unit;
+do not treat an HTTP response, missing exception, or actor return as endpoint
+success. No latency/call-efficiency comparison is performed.

+# Variables and scope
+
+| 記号/フィールド | 日本語の意味・定義 | SI単位 | 範囲・前提 | 型 |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| trial_id | 互いに独立した専用レコード対の試行識別子 | 非物理識別子 | C001〜C008 | 文字列 |
+| phase | 初回操作か、別意図による修復か | 非物理識別子 | initial / repair | 列挙文字列 |
+| namespace | この専用SQLiteファイルに限定した対象空間 | 非物理識別子 | owned-endpoint-5442-01a0ff58 | 文字列 |
+| target | 意図した文書とイベント上の実対象 | 非物理識別子 | A / B、意図はA | 列挙文字列 |
+| version | 保存レコードの更新世代、時間ではない | 1（無次元） | 整数0以上、本試行0〜3 | 整数スカラー |
+| desired_text/value | 意図した/実際に保存された文字列 | 非物理データ | 固定2文字列、初期値は空 | 文字列 |
+| nonce | 対象試行と操作段階を結ぶ一意識別子 | 非物理識別子 | 固定operation_idとphaseの結合 | 文字列 |
+| written | アプリが記録する書込み有無 | 1（無次元） | 0 / 1、真偽値とは区別 | 整数スカラー |
+| observed_ns | 同一プロセスのperf_counter_nsによる記録値 | ns（10^-9 s） | 単調時計、性能比較には使わない | 整数スカラー |
+| utc | 観測開始・終了とUIログの壁時計 | UTC日時、物理差の評価なし | ホスト時計、同期精度不明 | ISO文字列 |
+| semantic_status | 意図・実対象・前後版・実保存値の照合結果 | 非物理識別子 | CONFIRMED / UNKNOWN | 列挙文字列 |
+| HTTP status | 当該フォーム要求へのHTTP応答 | 非物理コード | 初回/修復は200を要求 | 整数スカラー |
+| SHA-256 | ソース/原出力のバイト識別 | 非物理識別子 | 64桁16進数、真正性証明ではない | 文字列 |
+
+SQLiteの別接続からの読取りは保存可視性を示す。停電耐性や外部アプリの真正な状態を示さない。
+UI操作回数14は構築の計画値であり、別方式とのモデル呼出し削減・待ち時間削減を実測していない。

+// Load into the existing CUA REPL; never invoke this through another browser API.
+// Parameters contain trial IDs and desired strings only, with no endpoint mode.
+async function runEndpointTrial(trialId, desiredText, phase) {
+  if (!['initial', 'repair'].includes(phase)) throw Error('invalid phase');
+  const key = trialId + ':' + phase;
+  if (semanticConsumed.has(key)) throw Error('consumed submit; no retry');
+  const record = async (stage, state, extra = {}) => {
+    await semanticFs.appendFile(semanticTrace, JSON.stringify({trial_id: trialId, phase,
+      stage, utc: new Date().toISOString(), state, ...extra}) + '\n', 'utf8');
+  };
+  await semanticTab.playwright.getByRole('link', {name: (phase === 'repair' ? 'Repair ' : 'Trial ') + trialId, exact: true}).click();
+  let state = await semanticTab.getAXState({emit: false, disableDiffing: true});
+  await record('editor', state);
+  const heading = (phase === 'repair' ? 'Fresh repair ' : 'Trial ') + trialId;
+  if (!state.includes(heading) || !state.includes('New value') || !state.includes('Target: Document A'))
+    throw Error('unexpected editor; HOLD');
+  await semanticTab.playwright.getByLabel('New value', {exact: true}).fill(desiredText);
+  state = await semanticTab.getAXState({emit: false, disableDiffing: true});
+  await record('intent-before-submit', state, {desired_text: desiredText, nonce: '5442-01a0ff58-' + trialId + ':' + phase});
+  if (!state.includes(desiredText)) throw Error('input value absent; HOLD before submit');
+  semanticConsumed.add(key);
+  try {
+    await semanticTab.playwright.getByRole('button', {name: phase === 'repair' ? 'Repair safely' : 'Save', exact: true}).click();
+    state = await semanticTab.getAXState({emit: false, disableDiffing: true});
+    await record('submit-returned', state);
+    if (!state.includes('Request processed') || !state.includes('Dispatcher accepted') || !state.includes('Trial ' + trialId))
+      throw Error('unexpected submit outcome; HOLD');
+    await semanticTab.playwright.getByRole('link', {name: 'All trials', exact: true}).click();
+    state = await semanticTab.getAXState({emit: false, disableDiffing: true});
+    await record('dashboard-returned', state);
+    if (!state.includes('Endpoint receipt experiment')) throw Error('unexpected dashboard; HOLD');
+    nodeRepl.write({trial_id: trialId, phase, ui: 'accepted'});
+  } catch (error) {
+    await record('HOLD-after-consumed-submit', state, {reason: String(error)});
+    throw error;
+  }
+}
diff --git a/research/integration/semantic_endpoint_browser_5442_01a0ff58/retained-source/app.py.txt b/research/integration/semantic_endpoint_browser_5442_01a0ff58/retained-source/app.py.txt

+++ b/research/integration/semantic_endpoint_browser_5442_01a0ff58/retained-source/app.py.txt
@@ -0,0 +1,196 @@
+"""Controlled local HTML form and persisted endpoint; no external dependencies."""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import html
+import json
+import os
+import sqlite3
+import time
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def initialize(path, scenarios):
+    if path.exists():
+        raise ValueError('existing database; no reset/restart')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.executescript('''
+        CREATE TABLE documents(room TEXT, target TEXT, version INTEGER, value TEXT,
+                               PRIMARY KEY(room,target));
+        CREATE TABLE events(room TEXT, phase TEXT, nonce TEXT UNIQUE,
+          requested_target TEXT, actual_target TEXT, applied_pre INTEGER,
+          applied_post INTEGER, written INTEGER, text TEXT, observed_ns INTEGER,
+          PRIMARY KEY(room,phase));
+        CREATE TABLE requests(sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+          method TEXT, path TEXT, status INTEGER, observed_ns INTEGER);
+        ''')
+        for item in scenarios:
+            db.executemany('INSERT INTO documents VALUES(?,?,0,?)',
+                           [(item['trial_id'], 'A', ''), (item['trial_id'], 'B', '')])
+        db.commit()
+
+
+def snapshot(path, room):
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        return {target: {'version': version, 'value': value}
+                for target, version, value in db.execute(
+                    'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=? ORDER BY target', (room,))}
+
+
+def apply(path, scenario, phase, value, expected=None):
+    room = scenario['trial_id']
+    nonce = scenario['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as db:
+        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
+        if db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE room=? AND phase=?', (room, phase)).fetchone():
+            raise ValueError('duplicate operation')
+        state = {t: (v, text) for t, v, text in db.execute(
+            'SELECT target,version,value FROM documents WHERE room=?', (room,))}
+        if phase == 'repair':
+            if expected != {t: state[t][0] for t in ('A', 'B')}:
+                raise ValueError('fresh-version comparison failed')
+            pre = state['A'][0]
+            if state['B'][1]:
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', ('', room, 'B'))
+            db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, room, 'A'))
+            target, written, post = 'A', 1, pre + 1
+        else:
+            target = 'B' if scenario['mode'] == 'wrong_target' else 'A'
+            if scenario['mode'] == 'stale_precondition':
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET version=version+1 WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, 'A'))
+            pre = db.execute('SELECT version FROM documents WHERE room=? AND target=?', (room, target)).fetchone()[0]
+            written = int(scenario['mode'] != 'noop')
+            post = pre + written
+            if written:
+                db.execute('UPDATE documents SET value=?,version=? WHERE room=? AND target=?', (value, post, room, target))
+        db.execute('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
+            (room, phase, nonce, 'A', target, pre, post, written, value, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+        db.commit()
+    return nonce
+
+
+def serve(out):
+    freeze = json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())
+    for name, digest in freeze['files'].items():
+        if hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
+            raise ValueError('source freeze drift:' + name)
+    scenarios = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+    by_id = {r['trial_id']: r for r in scenarios}
+    database = out / 'endpoint.sqlite'
+    initialize(database, scenarios)
+    (out / 'initial-database.sha256').write_text(hashlib.sha256(database.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + '\n')
+
+    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+        def request_log(self, status):
+            with closing(sqlite3.connect(database)) as db:
+                db.execute('INSERT INTO requests(method,path,status,observed_ns) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',
+                           (self.command, self.path, status, time.perf_counter_ns()))
+                db.commit()
+
+        def send_page(self, body, status=200):
+            self.request_log(status)
+            data = ('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Endpoint receipt test</title>'
+                '<style>body{font:16px system-ui;margin:30px;color:#172230}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}'
+                'th,td{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #ccd5dd;text-align:left}input,button{font:inherit;padding:9px}'
+                '.card{display:inline-block;vertical-align:top;padding:15px;background:#edf2f6;margin:8px 8px 20px 0}'
+                'h1{font-size:25px}h2{font-size:20px}.note{color:#536271}</style></head><body>' + body + '</body></html>').encode()
+            self.send_response(status)
+            self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8')
+            self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(data)))
+            self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; base-uri 'none'")
+            self.end_headers()
+            self.wfile.write(data)
+
+        def document_cards(self, room):
+            current = snapshot(database, room)
+            return ''.join('<section class="card"><h2>Document ' + target + '</h2><p>Version ' + str(row['version']) +
+                '</p><p>Saved value: ' + html.escape(row['value'] or '(empty)') + '</p></section>' for target, row in current.items())
+
+        def dashboard(self):
+            initial_file, repair_file = out / 'initial-observation.json', out / 'repair-observation.json'
+            initial = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(initial_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if initial_file.exists() else {}
+            repair = {r['trial_id']: r for r in json.loads(repair_file.read_bytes())['rows']} if repair_file.exists() else {}
+            rows = []
+            for scenario in scenarios:
+                room = scenario['trial_id']
+                state = snapshot(database, room)
+                observed = initial.get(room, {})
+                repaired = repair.get(room, {})
+                recovery = '<a href="/repair/' + room + '">Repair ' + room + '</a>' if observed.get('semantic_status') == 'UNKNOWN' and not repaired else ''
+                rows.append('<tr><td><a href="/trial/' + room + '">Trial ' + room + '</a></td><td>' +
+                    html.escape(scenario['desired_text']) + '</td><td>' + observed.get('dispatch_status', 'not run') +
+                    '</td><td>' + observed.get('semantic_status', 'not observed') + '</td><td>' +
+                    repaired.get('semantic_status', recovery or 'not needed') + '</td><td>' + html.escape(state['A']['value'] or '(empty)') + '</td></tr>')
+            return ('<h1>Endpoint receipt experiment</h1><p class="note">Private test records. Initial decisions remain unchanged after repair.</p>' +
+                '<table><tr><th>Trial</th><th>Goal value for A</th><th>Dispatch</th><th>Initial endpoint</th><th>Fresh repair</th><th>Saved A now</th></tr>' + ''.join(rows) + '</table>')
+
+        def editor(self, room, phase):
+            state = snapshot(database, room)
+            nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            hidden = ''.join('<input type="hidden" name="' + key + '" value="' + html.escape(str(value)) + '">' for key, value in {
+                'room': room, 'phase': phase, 'nonce': nonce, 'target': 'A',
+                'version_A': state['A']['version'], 'version_B': state['B']['version']}.items())
+            return ('<h1>' + ('Fresh repair ' if phase == 'repair' else 'Trial ') + room + '</h1><p>Target: Document A</p>' +
+                self.document_cards(room) + '<form action="/apply" method="post">' + hidden +
+                '<label for="value">New value</label><br><input id="value" name="value" maxlength="128" required><button type="submit">' +
+                ('Repair safely' if phase == 'repair' else 'Save') + '</button></form><p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>')
+
+        def do_GET(self):
+            path = urlparse(self.path).path
+            if path == '/':
+                self.send_page(self.dashboard())
+            elif path.startswith('/trial/') or path.startswith('/repair/'):
+                room = path.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
+                if room not in by_id:
+                    self.send_page('Unknown trial', 404)
+                    return
+                phase = 'repair' if path.startswith('/repair/') else 'initial'
+                self.send_page(self.editor(room, phase))
+            else:
+                self.send_page('Not found', 404)
+
+        def do_POST(self):
+            if self.path != '/apply' or self.headers.get('Origin') != 'http://127.0.0.1:' + str(self.server.server_port):
+                self.send_page('Refused origin or route', 403)
+                return
+            count = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', '0'))
+            if not 0 < count <= 2048:
+                self.send_page('Refused body size', 400)
+                return
+            fields = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(self.rfile.read(count).decode(), keep_blank_values=True).items()}
+            room, phase = fields.get('room'), fields.get('phase')
+            if room not in by_id or phase not in ('initial', 'repair') or fields.get('target') != 'A' or not 0 < len(fields.get('value', '')) <= 128:
+                self.send_page('Refused fields', 400)
+                return
+            wanted_nonce = by_id[room]['operation_id'] + (':repair' if phase == 'repair' else ':initial')
+            if fields.get('nonce') != wanted_nonce:
+                self.send_page('Refused nonce', 400)
+                return
+            try:
+                expected = {t: int(fields['version_' + t]) for t in ('A', 'B')}
+                apply(database, by_id[room], phase, fields['value'], expected)
+            except (ValueError, KeyError):
+                self.send_page('Refused duplicate or stale repair', 409)
+                return
+            self.send_page('<h1>Request processed</h1><p role="status">Dispatcher accepted</p><p>Trial ' + room +
+                '</p>' + self.document_cards(room) + '<p><a href="/">All trials</a></p>')
+
+    server = HTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), Handler)
+    metadata = {'pid': os.getpid(), 'port': server.server_port, 'host': '127.0.0.1',
+                'started_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'freeze_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                'sqlite_version': sqlite3.sqlite_version, 'instance_id': freeze['study']}
+    with (out / 'server.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+        json.dump(metadata, handle, sort_keys=True, indent=2)
+    server.serve_forever()
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    serve(args.out)

+"""Raw-only reimplementation. Imports neither the app nor its observer/actor."""
+import argparse
+import copy
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sqlite3
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def require(condition, explanation):
+    if not condition:
+        raise ValueError(explanation)
+
+
+def typed_equal(left, right):
+    if type(left) is not type(right):
+        return False
+    if type(right) is dict:
+        return set(left) == set(right) and all(typed_equal(left[k], right[k]) for k in right)
+    if type(right) is list:
+        return len(left) == len(right) and all(typed_equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(left, right))
+    return left == right
+
+
+def endpoint_truth(row, scenario, before=None):
+    event = row.get('event')
+    if type(event) is not dict or type(row.get('state')) is not dict:
+        return 'UNKNOWN'
+    phase = 'initial' if before is None else 'repair'
+    pre = 0 if before is None else before['A']['version']
+    expected_state = {'A': {'version': pre + 1, 'value': scenario['desired_text']},
+                      'B': {'version': 0 if before is None else before['B']['version'] + int(before['B']['value'] != ''), 'value': ''}}
+    wanted_intent = {k: scenario[k] for k in ('namespace', 'target', 'desired_text')}
+    wanted_intent.update(nonce=scenario['operation_id'] + ':' + phase, expected_pre_version=pre)
+    if before is not None:
+        wanted_intent.update(previous_state=before, expected_versions={t: r['version'] for t, r in before.items()})
+    selected = {k: event.get(k) for k in ('room', 'phase', 'nonce', 'requested_target', 'actual_target',
+                                       'applied_pre', 'applied_post', 'written', 'text')}
+    wanted_event = {'room': scenario['trial_id'], 'phase': phase, 'nonce': scenario['operation_id'] + ':' + phase,
+                    'requested_target': 'A', 'actual_target': 'A', 'applied_pre': pre, 'applied_post': pre + 1,
+                    'written': 1, 'text': scenario['desired_text']}
+    checks = (row.get('trial_id') == scenario['trial_id'], row.get('phase') == phase,
+              typed_equal(row.get('intent'), wanted_intent), typed_equal(selected, wanted_event),
+              typed_equal(row['state'], expected_state))
+    return 'CONFIRMED' if all(checks) else 'UNKNOWN'
+
+
+def check_raw(out, phase, scenarios, previous=None):
+    result = json.loads((out / (phase + '-observation.json')).read_bytes())
+    rows = result['rows']
+    chosen = [s for s in scenarios if phase == 'initial' or previous[s['trial_id']]['semantic_status'] == 'UNKNOWN']
+    require([r['trial_id'] for r in rows] == [s['trial_id'] for s in chosen], 'exact ordered row identity')
+    require(result['study'] == json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())['study'], 'study identity')
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect((out / ('endpoint-after-' + phase + '.sqlite')).as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)) as db:
+        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+        for table, ordering in [('documents', 'room,target'), ('events', 'room,phase'), ('requests', 'sequence')]:
+            require(typed_equal(result[table], [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM ' + table + ' ORDER BY ' + ordering)]), 'SQLite/raw disagreement:' + table)
+    answers = []
+    for row, scenario in zip(rows, chosen):
+        before = None if previous is None else previous[scenario['trial_id']]['state']
+        truth = endpoint_truth(row, scenario, before)
+        expected = 'CONFIRMED' if phase == 'repair' or scenario['mode'] == 'valid' else 'UNKNOWN'
+        require(truth == expected == row['semantic_status'], 'wrong semantic result:' + scenario['trial_id'])
+        state = {d['target']: {'version': d['version'], 'value': d['value']} for d in result['documents'] if d['room'] == row['trial_id']}
+        require(typed_equal(row['state'], state), 'detached document state')
+        events = [e for e in result['events'] if e['room'] == row['trial_id'] and e['phase'] == phase]
+        require(len(events) == 1 and typed_equal(row['event'], events[0]), 'detached/duplicate event')
+        require(row['dispatch_status'] == 'accepted', 'missing dispatch')
+        answers.append({'trial_id': row['trial_id'], 'phase': phase, 'truth': truth})
+    require(len(result['events']) == (8 if phase == 'initial' else 14), 'event cardinality')
+    posts = [r for r in result['requests'] if r['method'] == 'POST']
+    require(len(posts) == (8 if phase == 'initial' else 14) and all(r['status'] == 200 and r['path'] == '/apply' for r in posts), 'HTTP POST cardinality/status')
+    return result, answers
+
+
+def audit(out):
+    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
+    freeze = json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())
+    for name, digest in freeze['files'].items():
+        require(hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest, 'source drift:' + name)
+    scenarios = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+    first, answers = check_raw(out, 'initial', scenarios)
+    prior = {r['trial_id']: r for r in first['rows']}
+    second, more = check_raw(out, 'repair', scenarios, prior)
+    trace = [json.loads(s) for s in (out / 'ui-trace.jsonl').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()]
+    require(not any('HOLD' in r['stage'] for r in trace), 'UI HOLD')
+    require(len(trace) == 56, 'four trace entries per operation')
+    expected_operations = [(s['trial_id'], 'initial') for s in scenarios] + [(s['trial_id'], 'repair') for s in scenarios if s['mode'] != 'valid']
+    actual_operations = []
+    for index in range(0, len(trace), 4):
+        chunk = trace[index:index + 4]
+        require([r['stage'] for r in chunk] == ['editor', 'intent-before-submit', 'submit-returned', 'dashboard-returned'], 'UI sequence')
+        identity = (chunk[0]['trial_id'], chunk[0]['phase'])
+        require(all((r['trial_id'], r['phase']) == identity for r in chunk), 'UI mixed identity')
+        scenario = next(s for s in scenarios if s['trial_id'] == identity[0])
+        require(chunk[1]['desired_text'] == scenario['desired_text'] and chunk[1]['nonce'] == scenario['operation_id'] + ':' + identity[1], 'UI intent mismatch')
+        require('Dispatcher accepted' in chunk[2]['state'] and 'Request processed' in chunk[2]['state'], 'UI success absent')
+        actual_operations.append(identity)
+    require(actual_operations == expected_operations, 'UI exact operations')
+    baseline = first['rows'][0]
+    changes = [('actual_target', lambda r: r['event'].__setitem__('actual_target', 'B')),
+               ('intent_target', lambda r: r['intent'].__setitem__('target', 'B')),
+               ('preversion', lambda r: r['event'].__setitem__('applied_pre', 1)),
+               ('post_bool_alias', lambda r: r['event'].__setitem__('applied_post', True)),
+               ('saved_value', lambda r: r['state']['A'].__setitem__('value', 'other')),
+               ('missing_event', lambda r: r.__setitem__('event', None)),
+               ('collateral_B', lambda r: r['state']['B'].__setitem__('version', 1)),
+               ('event_text', lambda r: r['event'].__setitem__('text', 'other')),
+               ('nonce', lambda r: r['event'].__setitem__('nonce', 'other')),
+               ('namespace', lambda r: r['intent'].__setitem__('namespace', 'other'))]
+    mutations = []
+    for name, change in changes:
+        row = copy.deepcopy(baseline)
+        change(row)
+        decision = endpoint_truth(row, scenarios[0])
+        require(decision == 'UNKNOWN', 'mutation accepted:' + name)
+        mutations.append({'name': name, 'decision': decision, 'copied_row': row})
+    result = {'started_utc': started, 'ended_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
+              'source_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'initial_sha256': hashlib.sha256((out / 'initial-observation.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'repair_sha256': hashlib.sha256((out / 'repair-observation.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'ui_trace_sha256': hashlib.sha256((out / 'ui-trace.jsonl').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'raw_matches': answers + more, 'copied_raw_mutations': mutations, 'result': 'PASS_APPLICATION_ENDPOINT_SCOPED'}
+    with (out / 'AUDIT.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+        json.dump(result, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
+    print(json.dumps({'result': result['result'], 'raw_matches': len(answers + more), 'mutation_rejections': len(mutations)}))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    audit(args.out.resolve())

+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sqlite3
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+args = parser.parse_args()
+out = args.out.resolve()
+with closing(sqlite3.connect((out / 'endpoint.sqlite').as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)) as db:
+    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+    documents = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM documents ORDER BY room,target')]
+    events = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM events')]
+    requests = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM requests')]
+if len(documents) != 16 or events or requests or any(r['version'] != 0 or r['value'] != '' for r in documents):
+    raise ValueError('unexpected initial state; no GUI allowed')
+result = {'utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'documents': documents, 'events': events, 'requests': requests,
+          'database_sha256': hashlib.sha256((out / 'endpoint.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+with (out / 'PRESTATE.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+    json.dump(result, handle, indent=2)
+print(json.dumps({'rows': len(documents), 'events': 0, 'requests': 0}))

+"""Ordinary SQLite unit construction check, not a primary browser run."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+out = ROOT / 'construction-02'
+out.mkdir()
+started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
+source = (ROOT / 'app.py').read_bytes()
+(out / 'app.source.py.txt').write_bytes(source)
+check = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-m', 'py_compile', str(ROOT / 'app.py')], capture_output=True)
+(out / 'compile.stdout.txt').write_bytes(check.stdout)
+(out / 'compile.stderr.txt').write_bytes(check.stderr)
+assert check.returncode == 0
+import app
+items = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+db = out / 'construction.sqlite'
+app.initialize(db, items)
+checks = []
+for item in items:
+    room = item['trial_id']
+    assert app.snapshot(db, room) == {'A': {'version': 0, 'value': ''}, 'B': {'version': 0, 'value': ''}}
+    app.apply(db, item, 'initial', item['desired_text'])
+    state = app.snapshot(db, room)
+    mode = item['mode']
+    target = 'B' if mode == 'wrong_target' else 'A'
+    assert state[target]['value'] == ('' if mode == 'noop' else item['desired_text'])
+    assert state['A']['version'] == {'valid': 1, 'wrong_target': 0, 'noop': 0, 'stale_precondition': 2}[mode]
+    try:
+        app.apply(db, item, 'initial', item['desired_text'])
+    except ValueError:
+        pass
+    else:
+        raise AssertionError('duplicate accepted')
+    assert app.snapshot(db, room) == state
+    if mode != 'valid':
+        versions = {t: r['version'] for t, r in state.items()}
+        try:
+            app.apply(db, item, 'repair', item['desired_text'], {'A': -1, 'B': -1})
+        except ValueError:
+            pass
+        else:
+            raise AssertionError('stale repair accepted')
+        assert app.snapshot(db, room) == state
+        app.apply(db, item, 'repair', item['desired_text'], versions)
+        after = app.snapshot(db, room)
+        assert after['A'] == {'version': versions['A'] + 1, 'value': item['desired_text']}
+        assert after['B']['value'] == ''
+        assert after['B']['version'] == versions['B'] + int(bool(state['B']['value']))
+    checks.append({'trial_id': room, 'mode': mode, 'passed': True})
+result = {'started_utc': started, 'ended_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
+          'app_sha256': hashlib.sha256(source).hexdigest(), 'compile_exit': check.returncode,
+          'unit_scenarios': checks, 'duplicate_guard_checks': 8, 'stale_guard_checks': 6,
+          'fresh_repair_checks': 6, 'GUI_actions': 0, 'primary_server_invocations': 0}
+(out / 'RESULT.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))

+import hashlib
+import json
+import platform
+import sqlite3
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path

+"""Separate-process endpoint observation, using fresh read-only SQLite connections."""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sqlite3
+from contextlib import closing
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def exact_integer(value, wanted):
+    return type(value) is int and value == wanted
+
+
+def decide(row):
+    intent, event, state = row['intent'], row['event'], row['state']
+    if not event:
+        return 'UNKNOWN'
+    initial = row['phase'] == 'initial'
+    pre = 0 if initial else intent['expected_versions']['A']
+    before_b = {'version': 0, 'value': ''} if initial else intent['previous_state']['B']
+    expected_b = {'version': before_b['version'] + (0 if initial else int(bool(before_b['value']))), 'value': ''}
+    conditions = (
+        intent['namespace'] == 'owned-endpoint-5442-01a0ff58',
+        intent['target'] == 'A', event['requested_target'] == intent['target'],
+        event['actual_target'] == intent['target'], event['room'] == row['trial_id'],
+        event['phase'] == row['phase'], event['nonce'] == intent['nonce'],
+        exact_integer(event['applied_pre'], pre), exact_integer(event['applied_post'], pre + 1),
+        exact_integer(event['written'], 1), event['text'] == intent['desired_text'],
+        set(state) == {'A', 'B'}, exact_integer(state['A']['version'], pre + 1),
+        state['A']['value'] == intent['desired_text'],
+        exact_integer(state['B']['version'], expected_b['version']), state['B']['value'] == '',
+    )
+    return 'CONFIRMED' if all(conditions) else 'UNKNOWN'
+
+
+def observe(out, phase):
+    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
+    freeze = json.loads((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes())
+    for name, digest in freeze['files'].items():
+        assert hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest, name
+    scenarios = json.loads((ROOT / 'scenarios.json').read_bytes())
+    initial = json.loads((out / 'initial-observation.json').read_bytes()) if phase == 'repair' else None
+    previous = {r['trial_id']: r for r in initial['rows']} if initial else {}
+    chosen = [s for s in scenarios if phase == 'initial' or previous[s['trial_id']]['semantic_status'] == 'UNKNOWN']
+    rows = []
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect((out / 'endpoint.sqlite').as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)) as db:
+        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+        db.execute('BEGIN')
+        documents = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM documents ORDER BY room,target')]
+        events = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM events ORDER BY room,phase')]
+        requests = [dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT * FROM requests ORDER BY sequence')]
+        for item in chosen:
+            room = item['trial_id']
+            state = {r['target']: {'version': r['version'], 'value': r['value']} for r in documents if r['room'] == room}
+            found = [r for r in events if r['room'] == room and r['phase'] == phase]
+            assert len(found) <= 1
+            intent = {k: item[k] for k in ('namespace', 'target', 'desired_text')}
+            intent['nonce'] = item['operation_id'] + ':' + phase
+            intent['expected_pre_version'] = 0
+            if phase == 'repair':
+                intent['previous_state'] = previous[room]['state']
+                intent['expected_versions'] = {t: r['version'] for t, r in previous[room]['state'].items()}
+                intent['expected_pre_version'] = intent['expected_versions']['A']
+            row = {'trial_id': room, 'phase': phase, 'intent': intent, 'event': found[0] if found else None,
+                   'state': state, 'dispatch_status': 'accepted' if found else 'missing'}
+            row['semantic_status'] = decide(row)
+            rows.append(row)
+        db.rollback()
+    result = {'study': freeze['study'], 'phase': phase, 'started_utc': started,
+              'ended_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
+              'freeze_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'FREEZE.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'observer_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+              'rows': rows, 'documents': documents, 'events': events, 'requests': requests,
+              'authority_scope': 'owned SQLite rows; same authored system, distinct process/read-only connection'}
+    with (out / (phase + '-observation.json')).open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
+        json.dump(result, handle, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
+    with closing(sqlite3.connect((out / 'endpoint.sqlite').as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)) as source:
+        destination = out / ('endpoint-after-' + phase + '.sqlite')
+        assert not destination.exists()
+        with closing(sqlite3.connect(destination)) as copy:
+            source.backup(copy)
+    print(json.dumps({'phase': phase, 'rows': len(rows), 'confirmed': sum(r['semantic_status'] == 'CONFIRMED' for r in rows)}, ensure_ascii=False))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
+    parser.add_argument('--phase', required=True, choices=('initial', 'repair'))
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    observe(args.out.resolve(), args.phase)

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..157175999ff732dcdef53a6aa5ce99dd8393283d

+"""Capture first ordinary subprocess output, return code and UTC boundaries."""
+import argparse
+import json
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--record-dir', type=Path, required=True)
+parser.add_argument('command', nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)
+args = parser.parse_args()
+args.record_dir.mkdir()
+command = args.command[1:] if args.command[:1] == ['--'] else args.command
+started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
+result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True)
+(args.record_dir / 'stdout.txt').write_bytes(result.stdout)
+(args.record_dir / 'stderr.txt').write_bytes(result.stderr)
+(args.record_dir / 'RESULT.json').write_text(json.dumps({'command': command, 'started_utc': started,
+    'ended_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'returncode': result.returncode}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(result.stdout.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
+if result.stderr:
+    print(result.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'), file=sys.stderr)
+sys.exit(result.returncode)
